# Overnight polish log
One line per shipped iteration — newest at the bottom. (Started when Ezra went to bed.)

- v2.31 — uid() now appends a random suffix: layer ids from different sessions can no longer collide, which could silently cross-link/destroy media between projects in the shared IndexedDB store.
- v2.32 — audio-only clips (mp3/wav) now show a music-note thumbnail in the timeline instead of a blank box.
- v2.33 — group selection box now hugs the members' actual bounds (was a meaningless 100px square at the corner); tapping grouped content on canvas selects the group as one object, and inside Edit Group the same tap picks the individual member.
- v2.34 — effect search now matches category names and ids too: typing "3d", "warp" or "blur" surfaces the whole family, not just effects with the word in their title.
- v2.35 — Wiggle/Drift/Orbit/Tiles/Raster-Extrude skip the per-frame full-canvas pixel scan they never used: those effects now cost a fraction of what they did during playback.
- v2.36 — home-screen project cards show their layer count in the meta line (fills in as each project is next opened/saved).
- v2.37 — hardened the boot media-cleanup against a race: it now stands down while template/duplicate media writes are in flight and re-verifies every candidate right before deleting, so fast actions at startup can never lose a clip.
- v2.38 — groups gained Color & Fill (full colour grade) and Border & Shadow (silhouette outline + drop shadow) — property parity with normal layers complete.
- v2.39 — add-menu rebuilt to match Alight Motion: AI Scene moved into Media, shapes now PAGE SIDEWAYS with page dots (no vertical scroll), tabs are Shape/Media/Audio/Object/Template/Freehand Drawing with Vector Drawing + Text on the right rail. NEW: Freehand Drawing (draw a brush stroke on the canvas) and Vector Drawing (tap points → filled shape), both real path layers you can restyle.
- v2.40 — Freehand Drawing moved off the hidden top tab onto the instant rail beside Text and Vector Drawing, so it's findable on mobile and all three spawn-instantly tools share one row on PC (like AM).
- v2.41 — shapes now come out identical in every project format: the default box is square (sized off the shorter side) instead of width/3 × height/3, which used to stretch a circle into an oval in 16:9 / 9:16.
- v2.42 — new clips (shapes, text, drawings, imported media) now spawn AT THE PLAYHEAD instead of at 0, and the timeline length auto-fits the clips: it grows when a clip extends past the end and shrinks when clips end earlier (e.g. 3s of clips = a 3s timeline).
- v2.43 — inspector cleanup: removed the redundant header row (thumbnail/rename/duplicate/delete); the orphan gap in the category grid is gone (a lone card now spans full width on PC); delete moved to the top bar next to ⋯/Export; and the top-bar name field now shows the selected layer's name (rename it there) and reverts to the project name when nothing's selected.
- v2.44 — added an Ungroup entry to the ⋯ three-dots menu (shows only when a group is selected); fixed the glitch where tapping a layer scrolled the timeline back to the top (vertical scroll is now preserved across the rebuild).
- v2.45 — reworked Color & Fill to match Alight Motion exactly: a 4-tab rail (None / Solid / Gradient / Media). Solid now fills the whole shape with one flat colour (swatch palette + hex + fill-opacity) — no more accidental gradient. Gradient is its own tab (Linear/Radial/Angular + live preview bar + two stops). Media fills the shape with a picture (cover-fit, clipped, stored as a downscaled data URL so it survives reload). Fill/gradient moved out of "Edit Shape" (now geometry+stroke only); text gets Solid+Gradient; lines/arcs just get one Colour tab. Old projects render unchanged (fill mode is derived from legacy fields).
- v2.46 — Color & Fill nailed down: (1) picked colours now render EXACTLY (a stale hue/sat grade from the old colour-wheel panel was silently shifting every fill — grades no longer touch fill-owned colours); (2) colour KEYFRAMING — a ◇ diamond next to the hex readout animates fill/text colour over time (channel-lerped, shows/deletes on the timeline like any keyframe, auto-keys once armed); (3) fill works on EVERYTHING — videos/images get the same None/Solid/Gradient/Media tabs and a solid pick fully overwrites the clip (None restores it; Colour Tune/grade tools stay available under None); groups recolour their whole silhouette; (4) shapes spawn with natural proportions (rectangle 3:2, arrow/line/semicircle elongated, circle/star square) and identical sizes in every project format.
- v2.47 — the massive one: (1) 37 new AM shapes (speech, moon, snowflake, shield, check, droplet, cloud, play, spiral, sparkle, stamp, bolt, puzzle, pushpin, flag, thumbs-up, paper plane, house, laurel, bookmark, pointing hand, flame, banner, wreath, diamond, plane, umbrella, bomb, boat, magnifier, key, sun, person, rocket, envelope, woman, car) — all in the add menu with icons generated from their real geometry; (2) EVERY shape has editable points — "✎ Edit points" in Edit Shape converts any shape to a path with draggable vertices on the canvas (drag to reshape, tap a ring to add a point, double-tap to delete); (3) Blending & Opacity rebuilt AM-style — opacity slider on top, then Normal/Darken/Lighten/Contrast/Difference/Color/Mask as expandable dropdowns (one mode each for now; Difference has Exclusion, Mask has include/exclude which truly mask the layers below — verified pixel-exact); (4) PC inspector: Effects is now the big full-width card at the bottom, everything else is a square (no more giant Blending card, no orphan gaps).
- v2.48 — shape grid is icon-only (names moved to tooltips): bigger 34px icons, 5-across on phone / 6-across on PC like AM. Upkeep sweep on top: paste-style no longer shares one colour-keyframe object across layers; nulls/adjustments/audio-only clips stop offering Color & Fill / Border cards that couldn't render; spiral now shows the single Colour tab (it's a stroked line, gradients did nothing); the point editor cleanly dismisses itself if its layer is deleted or the project switches mid-edit. Verified: full save→reload round trip of every new field (edited-path subs, angular gradient, fill opacity, colour keyframes, mask blend), duplicate carries them without aliasing, console clean.
- v2.49 — Motion Blur (Content): a NEW effect that blurs what moves INSIDE the clip (real optical-flow motion blur, like RSMB / AE Pixel Motion Blur) — a swinging sword now streaks across the frame while the background stays razor sharp. Four styles: Pixel (per-pixel directional blur along a block-matched flow field, coarse+fine search reaching very fast motion), Smear (one dominant direction, moving areas only), Echo (long-exposure light trails, AE-style lighten merge), Blend (frame blending — rescues low-fps clips). Settings: Style, Shutter, Quality, Threshold, Softness. Featured in the effects carousel; works on video, images, shapes, text and groups; identical in export. Verified: symmetric 0→128→0 smear profile across a moving object's path, static content untouched, decaying echo trail, ~19ms/frame preview cost.
- v2.50 — five fixes: (1) grabbing/scrolling/wheeling the timeline while playing now PAUSES playback (detected on the raw input — the playhead's own auto-scroll was swallowing user scrolls); (2) effect-detail sliders no longer get "stuck to the mouse" after release (the drag-back-to-zero bug — fxScrubber was the one scrubber missing the buttons-released guard); (3) Motion Blur (Content) un-broken: repaints at a paused playhead (every slider tweak causes one) collapsed the blur to a sharp frame, so the effect looked dead while you adjusted it — a two-slot plate cache keeps the blur visible; (4) home screen orders projects most-recently-edited first; (5) pointing-hand shape redrawn (thumb, extended finger, three knuckles — actually looks like ☞); (6) timeline paint-select is now a RANGE — drag down to add rows, drag back the way you came and they unselect again, no manual deselecting.
- v2.51 — the ACTUAL hand fix: redrew the thumbs-up (cuff, curved thumb with rounded tip, four arc-scalloped finger ends — reads as 👍 not a mitten) and gave the pointing hand the same rounded treatment (v2.50 had only touched the pointing one). Blending & Opacity is now a true accordion: opening one category closes whichever was open, tap again to close it — only one dropdown at a time.
- v2.52 — SPEED RAMPING + easing everywhere: the Speed panel gets the Move & Transform treatment — a ◆ keyframe button and an easing-curve button on a left rail. Keyframe the speed at two points and playback accelerates/decelerates along the eased curve between them (real time-remapping: video frames, scrubbing AND exported audio all follow the same integral, so sound stays locked to picture and pitch bends with the ramp — clip length stays fixed once ramped). Speed keyframes show/delete on the timeline like any other. Plus: EVERY effect parameter now has its own easing-curve button next to its keyframe diamond, opening the same full curve editor Move & Transform uses.
- v2.53 — the timeline is FRAME-ACCURATE end to end (re-landed + finished): the ruler notches are real frames (fully zoomed in = one notch per frame, exactly one per frame verified); dragging/scrubbing/positioning the playhead snaps frame-to-frame (playback stays smooth); pausing lands ON a frame; keyframes, benchmarks/markers and splits all live on the exact frame under the playhead. NEW: the skip buttons are keyframe-aware — when the playhead is on the selected clip they step through its keyframes (plus edges/benchmarks); off the clip they ignore keyframes. Bonus fix: splitting a speed-RAMPED clip now trims both halves through the ramp integral (was flat-speed math → wrong source position).
- v2.54 — the queued batch: (1) smooth scrubbing again — while your finger owns the timeline the playhead no longer yanks the strip back to the frame pixel mid-swipe (that fight was the jagged/stuck-at-high-zoom feel); it settles onto the exact notch when you let go, and time still steps per frame; (2) benchmarks toggle only on the SAME frame (0.12s window meant the next frame's benchmark deleted the previous one); (3) dragging a clip on the canvas between two keyframes now correctly drops a NEW keyframe on the current frame (same scroll-desync bug in disguise); (4) AM's layer top bar: a parent-picker button (link any layer to another — None/list dropdown) + the ⋯ menu is now AM's layer menu when a clip is selected — Save to My Elements, Flip Horizontally/Vertically (in place, keyframes untouched), Fit/Fill/Stretch to Composition Area, Create Clipping Mask, Convert to Outline, Extract Audio (video → its own audio layer, original muted), Media Info, quick-colour swatch strip, and "Project options…" to reach the old menu.
- v2.55 — four big ones: (1) the layer colour swatch now adds a small COLOUR TAG stripe on the layer header (far left), not changing the fill; (2) Motion Blur (Content) fixed to blur only what moves INSIDE the layer — moving/zooming/rotating the clip to reframe it no longer smears it (renders the content at a neutral transform, blurs there, then re-applies your transform); (3) AUTO HEAD/MOTION TRACKING in Move & Transform (video layers): tap the head on the canvas, hit Track, and it writes position keyframes that follow it — keeping it pinned where you tapped; the keyframes are normal keyframes you then drag to touch up; (4) COPY BACKGROUND effect — put it on a full-screen shape and it shows a live copy of everything below, so any effects/colour-correction you add on top cover the whole scene (a maskable, animatable adjustment layer).
- v2.56 — hardening pass from an adversarial test workflow (found 10 real issues, all fixed): CRITICAL — Copy Background was saving its live backdrop canvas into the project, which corrupted the file on reload/duplicate/import; runtime fields (canvas snapshots, caches) are now stripped from every save. Motion Blur (Content) on a GROUP no longer renders only a clipped corner; it no longer loses a feathered mask's soft edge; and it no longer shifts a layer that has an off-centre anchor. Copy Background is now offered only on shapes (was a silent no-op on video/text). Auto-track now refuses parented layers (the button hides + a toast) since it can't account for a parent's motion; its frame budget scales with clip length (long clips no longer degrade to a near-static track) and its keyframe simplifier is iterative (no stack overflow on long tracks). The tracking overlay tears down cleanly if you delete the layer or switch projects mid-pick. Layer colour-tag stripe is now an absolute left-edge marker that never nudges the mobile layer header.
- v2.57 — three fixes: (1) the layer headers on the left now stay pinned on screen when you scroll the timeline (the v2.55 colour-tag CSS had accidentally overridden their sticky-left pin); (2) Copy Background works on EVERY layer type now, not just shapes — it fills the layer's footprint (a shape's outline, a text layer's glyphs, or a video/image's frame) with the backdrop copy, so effects on top cover the scene; (3) clicking off a clip and back on it now reopens at the category grid, not the sub-menu you last had open (e.g. you won't land back inside the Effects list).
- v2.58 — swipe-to-delete on effects rebuilt to feel like iOS: swiping an effect row now slides it left to REVEAL a red delete panel with a trash icon (the icon pops when you've pulled far enough), instead of the row just vanishing. It's much easier to trigger — a quick flick deletes even on a short swipe (velocity-aware), a firm pull deletes, and a small drag springs back with a bouncy ease. On delete the row's height collapses so the list closes the gap smoothly instead of snapping. Rubber-banding on both ends so it never feels like it hits a wall.
- v2.59 — keyboard/mouse polish: (1) number keys open the Add menu tabs — 1 Shape · 2 Media · 3 Audio · 4 Object/Element · 5 Template — and Shift+1/2/3 instantly add Text / Freehand Drawing / Vector Drawing; (2) on PC, clicking anywhere off the inspector now steps the open sub-menu back (effects → grid → deselect) so you can dismiss it by clicking the sides; (3) Escape now goes BACK a page instead of closing outright — from the effects page it returns to the category grid, Escape again deselects (and it first bails out of the shortcuts overlay / point-edit / tracking pick). Every shortcut (incl. these + swipe-to-delete + click-off) is now listed in the ? Shortcuts & tips panel.
- v2.60 — swipe-to-delete fixes: (1) it now deletes on a SHORT swipe (~18% of the row / under 56px) or any quick flick — no more dragging it halfway across; (2) fixed the stuck-drag where releasing the mouse then moving back over the row kept swiping it — a button-released guard now ends the gesture the instant the button is up (same fix the sliders got).
- v2.61 — three: (1) pressing Enter now finishes a Vector Drawing (same as Done); Escape cancels it. (2) With a layer selected, number keys open its category panels — 1 Color & Fill, 2 Border & Shadow, 3 Blending, 4 Move & Transform, 5 Edit Shape, 6 Presets, 7 Effects (order matches the cards) — and each card now shows its number in the corner so you can remember. (With nothing selected, 1-5 still open the Add-menu tabs; Shift+1/2/3 still add Text/Freehand/Vector.) (3) A new/empty project is now 0 seconds instead of 10 — the timeline is only as long as its clips, growing as you add them and shrinking back to 0 when emptied.
- v2.62 — (1) clicking anywhere off the inspector on PC now fully CLOSES it (deselects back to the Add menu), no matter how deep you were — e.g. the Effects sub-menu; Esc stays the gentle step-back (effects → grid → deselect). (2) The timeline now has momentum: flick-drag it and it keeps gliding after you let go, decelerating to a smooth stop and settling on a frame (like flicking a scroll on a website). A deliberate slow drag doesn't fling, and grabbing / playing / clicking stops the glide instantly.
- v2.63 — mobile fix: the empty-timeline hint ("No layers yet — Import media, add Text, Captions, or a Sample clip.") no longer runs off the right edge of the screen. It lives inside the horizontally-scrolling track area so it had inherited that area's full width (704px on a 375px phone) and the text was clipped; it's now pinned sticky to the visible viewport and width-capped, so it wraps on-screen and reads in full.
- v2.64 — pasting a layer now drops it at the PLAYHEAD, not back on the source clip's original start time (matches Alight Motion). Paste several clips at once and they keep their relative spacing, anchored so the earliest one lands on the playhead; the timeline auto-grows if a pasted clip runs past the end.
- v2.65 — keyframe UX fixes (apply to EVERY keyframe editor — Move & Transform, effects, colour, volume, speed): (1) auto-keying is no longer invisible — changing a value at a new playhead time on an already-animated property inserts a keyframe AND the dot now shows on the timeline immediately (setTransform/setProp refresh on insert); (2) new keyframes default to a STRAIGHT (linear) graph instead of slow-in/slow-out; (3) the ◆ keyframe button turns amber whenever the playhead is sitting on an existing keyframe (press = delete it) and stays teal otherwise (press = add a new one) — now consistent across Move & Transform, colour and every other editor.
- v2.66 — Edit Shape rework to match Alight Motion: (1) NEW — normal clips (photo/video) now get an "Edit Shape" panel that is a SIZE editor, just like AM: pixel Width/Height boxes, the two top-right toggles (Aspect Ratio Locked ⇄ Resize Freely, and Size from center ⇄ Size from edge), and a ◆ keyframe + easing-curve rail so you can animate size. Aspect-locked resizes proportionally; free resizes each axis on its own; size-from-edge keeps the top-left corner fixed while size-from-center keeps the anchor fixed. (2) PRUNE — removed everything from Edit Shape that isn't in AM's Edit Shape: Motion blur, Wiggle, Mask and the Start/Duration rows are gone for shapes, text and media (timing is set on the timeline; masking still lives in Blending's mask modes + masking groups). Old projects that already used those fields keep rendering them.
- v2.67 — added Bounce and Elastic easing presets to the keyframe graph editor (they were missing — AM has them). Unlike the cubic-bezier presets, these are real oscillating curves: Bounce settles like a dropped ball, Elastic springs past and wobbles back. They show as their own preset buttons with correct mini-glyphs, draw their true curve in the graph, animate + export identically (evalProp resolves them), and the AI scene builder can use them too (they're in EASE_NAMES). Works on every keyframe editor that opens the curve — Move & Transform, effects, colour, volume, speed, and the new Edit Shape size.
- v2.68 — Edit Shape for media is now a real CROP editor (was wrongly scaling): Width/Height crop the visible frame in source pixels — the photo/video content stays the same size, you just see less of it (nothing is stretched or shrunk). The two AM controls (aspect-ratio lock + size-from-edge/center) now sit at the TOP-RIGHT of the Edit Shape header where AM puts them, and there's a ◆ keyframe + easing rail so the crop can animate. NEW (not in AM): a "Free crop" button — tap it and drag a box right on the playback area, iPhone-photo-style (dimmed outside, 8 handles, rule-of-thirds grid, Reset/Cancel/Done). Crop saves/reloads cleanly; the live edit flag never leaks into the project file.
- v2.69 — bug fixes found while the deploy was stuck: (1) Copy Background on a CROPPED photo/video now covers only the crop, not the whole frame (its footprint was still using the full source size); (2) the Free Crop tool no longer leaks window event listeners each time you open+close it (and cancels any pending redraw on teardown); (3) merely opening Edit Shape on a clip no longer stamps a redundant crop field into the project — the crop is only created when you actually crop. (Also traced a stubborn GitHub Pages "Deployment failed, try again later" backend error — the build was fine, GitHub's deploy side was erroring.)
- v2.70 — the timeline is now a REAL fixed-time-scale timeline (ripped out the "fit-to-viewport" hack): a 1-second clip is physically 1 second wide and STAYS that width when you trim it — no more springing back to full width. An empty/new project is a genuine 0-second timeline (no phantom 5-second scaffold, nothing to scroll to). And the length is baked in: it's recomputed from the clips on every single timeline redraw, so it can never get stuck at the wrong length — it grows when a clip extends past the end and shrinks the instant you trim or delete. Zoom, scrub, the centre playhead and end-of-timeline all still line up exactly.
- v2.71 — EDIT POINTS, built in properly: (1) every shape outline now supports two point types — CORNER (hard bend, square handle) and CURVE (the outline flows smoothly through it, round handle) — so round shapes stay round with only a handful of points instead of a 24-dot mess; (2) all 44 library shapes re-authored around editing: a point at every real bend/joint and nowhere else — the stick figure has shoulder/elbow/wrist/hip/knee/ankle points (move the arm points and he waves), his head is finally proportional, both hands redrawn cleaner, moon/flame/stamp fixed; (3) shapes with points show an "Edit Points" card REPLACING Edit Shape (like AM) which opens the editor directly: tap a point to select (green), drag it on the canvas or nudge with X/Y + the swipe pad, Curve/Corner buttons, ⊖ delete (or double-tap), tap a hollow ring to add a point ON the curve; (4) squares/circles and other parametric shapes (rect, ellipse, polygon, star, line, arc, pie, semicircle, ring) deliberately get NO points — they keep their clean Edit Shape sliders; (5) drawn freehand/vector paths use the same Edit Points editor; smooth flags save/reload/export identically.
- v2.72 — Edit Points sanity pass: audited every shape's point layout and pulled the five that were point-editing nightmares — snowflake (60 points), laurel (76), wreath (84), stamp (52) and sun (32). These are decorative/symmetric shapes made of many repeated leaves/spokes/rays, so editing individual points was pointless and messy; they now just resize like a normal shape (Edit Shape) instead of offering points. Organic shapes where the points are meaningful (the person's joints, hands, heart, cloud, etc.) keep Edit Points. Verified real on-canvas dragging is 1:1 precise and only moves the grabbed point.
- v2.73 — bezier curve handles in Edit Points (matches your AM screenshot): select a curve point and it now shows two tangent handles — drag either one to shape the curve exactly, instead of relying on the auto-smoothing. Handles are symmetric (drag one, the other mirrors) for smooth flow; the handle you haven't touched keeps auto-smoothing, so you only fiddle where you want to. Tap Curve to reset a point back to auto, Corner to make it a hard bend. Handles only show on the selected point (stays clean), and they save/reload/export with the shape.
- v2.74 — better Export dialog: the Resolution menu now offers named presets built from YOUR project's size — Full plus 720p / 480p / 360p (etc.) — each showing the exact output pixels (e.g. "720p — 720×1280"), instead of vague "Half / 75%". Only downscales are offered (no blurry upscaling), and it adapts to whatever canvas size the project uses. Frame rate gained 25 fps (PAL), 50 fps, and 12 fps alongside 24/30/60.
- v2.75 — EYEDROPPER (colour sampler) in every colour picker: tap the little pipette next to any colour (fill, stroke, gradient stops, effect colours) and then tap anywhere on the video — it grabs that exact colour from the rendered frame. iOS-style magnifier loupe shows the zoomed pixels + live hex as you move, so you can land on the right pixel. Works on iPhone/Safari (it samples the canvas directly, since Safari doesn't support the browser's built-in eyedropper). Esc cancels.
- v2.76 — CUSTOM FONT IMPORT: the Font dropdown (Text layer → Edit Text) now ends with "＋ Import font…" — pick a .ttf / .otf / .woff off your device and it's registered instantly and rendered on the canvas. Imported fonts collect under a "My fonts" group in the picker and are a GLOBAL library — import once, they show up in every project. They survive reload: the font file is stored in IndexedDB (not the 5MB localStorage) and re-registered via the FontFace API on boot, so your text keeps its font after a refresh or reopen. Saving a project file (.fmotion.json) now embeds the custom fonts its text actually uses, so the project renders correctly if you open it on another device. Verified end-to-end: import → registers + renders (Chalkduster on canvas) → survives a full reload → picker + mobile layout clean → console clean.
- v2.77 — TEXT EDITING REBUILT to match Alight Motion (from your screenshots): tapping a text layer's Edit Text (or adding new text) now opens a focused text-edit MODE — a big text field docked above the keyboard with a green focus border, and a compact top toolbar of exactly four controls: Align · Font · Size · Colour, plus a ✓ Done. The Font control is now a horizontal strip of live "Abc" previews each shown IN that font (built-ins + your imported fonts + an Import tile), just like AM — no more plain dropdown. Size is a slider; Colour opens the swatch/hex/eyedropper picker. Adding text jumps straight into the editor with the placeholder pre-selected. The advanced typography AM keeps OUT of that bar (Bold/Italic, Spacing, Line-height, Curve, Outline, Animate, Captions) stays in the inspector's Edit Text panel behind an "✎ Edit text" button — nothing lost. New js/text-edit.js overlay (keyboard-docked via visualViewport). Verified at 375px: live typing, font rail previews, size slider, colour+eyedropper, align cycle, Done commits as one undo + tears down clean, console clean.
- v2.78 — TEXT EDITING is now a real SECTION, not a one-time popup (your correction): the new editor IS the "Edit Text" section. Adding text jumps you straight into it (a shortcut); tapping "Edit Text" on an existing layer opens the same editor; ✓/back returns to the normal category grid (Color & Fill, Border & Shadow, Effects…). The old junk that was piled into Edit Text is gone from there: Outline moved to Border & Shadow (it's a border); the rest (Bold/Italic, Spacing, Line-height, Curve, Animate, Captions) tucked behind an "Aa" button inside the editor so the top bar stays exactly like your screenshots (Align · Font · Size · Colour · ✓). Verified at 375px: add→editor, Done→grid, Edit Text tile→editor, Aa sheet opens/rebuilds, Outline lives in Border & Shadow, text renders, console clean.
- v2.79 — text editor now dismisses when you click/tap off it (you reported it stuck around). Tap anywhere outside the editor: if a Font/Size/Colour/Aa popover is open it closes that first, otherwise it commits your text and drops you back on the category grid. Tapping inside the editor (the text field, the toolbar) keeps it open as expected. Eyedropper taps are left alone so colour-sampling still works.
- v2.80 — text editor is now a proper full-screen takeover on mobile (you reported the effects/border/move-transform grid + timeline poking through and not fitting). While editing text, the inspector category grid and the timeline are hidden and the canvas fills the freed space, centered between the top toolbar and the docked text field — just the editor, nothing else. Exiting restores the normal layout.
- v2.81 — bug-fix pass (found + adversarially verified via a multi-agent sweep). Fixed: (1) DESKTOP layout was broken by the v2.80 full-screen takeover (the #app grid override wasn't scoped to mobile, so on PC it nuked the top bar + timeline) — now mobile-only. (2) Deleting a text layer while its editor was open left a dead editor floating — now it closes. (3) "Use as caption track" from inside the editor lost your typed text (the editor wrote the old text back) — now it commits + closes the editor first, so the caption keeps what you typed. (4) A failed custom-font load was marked "done" and never retried on later reloads — now only marked on success. (5) The colour picker inside the editor could write to a stale layer after an undo — now it re-reads the live layer each time. (6) Opening the editor now closes the home screen / effects browser so it can't be hidden behind them.
- v2.82 — text editor keyboard fixes (from your iPhone screenshots): (1) the keyboard now opens automatically when you tap into Edit Text — the editor focuses the field synchronously inside your tap, which is the only way iOS pops the keyboard (before, the edit path deferred through a timer so iOS ignored it). (2) When the keyboard opens, the top toolbar (Align/Font/Size/Colour) now STAYS pinned at the top instead of getting shoved off — it tracks the visible viewport as iOS scrolls. The canvas also lifts above the keyboard + field so your text stays visible while typing. NOTE: these are iOS-Safari behaviours I can't fully simulate in the desktop preview — verified the synchronous focus + layout logic; the real test is your phone.
- v2.83 — BORDER & SHADOW rebuilt to match Alight Motion, with WORKING KEYFRAMES (Phase A). Border now applies to shapes AND text with a Position control (Inside / Center / Outside) plus Colour and Size — and Size + Colour have real keyframe diamonds, so you can animate a border growing or changing colour over time. Shadow gained Alpha (opacity %) and Position X/Y alongside Colour and Size, all keyframeable (animate a shadow fading or sliding). The diamonds tie into the same keyframe engine as Move & Transform, so ticks show/drag/delete on the timeline. Verified: border width animates 6→23→40 across time, shadow alpha animates 100→50→0, all three border positions render correctly, panel matches the AM layout at 375px. STILL TO COME: multiple borders (+ Add Border) = Phase B; animated draw-on Stroke (Start/End) = Phase C.
- v2.84 — MOBILE PERF Phase 1 (smooth scrubbing + fewer stalls; from PERF-PLAN.md). Three low-risk wins: (B) Timeline scrubbing is now COALESCED — a fast finger fires pointermove 60–120×/sec, and each event used to run a full synchronous compositor render + a video seek. New FM.scrubTime batches both to ≤1 per animation frame (same proven pattern the inspector sliders use); playhead + timecode still update instantly so the line tracks your finger. Verified: 50 scrub calls → 1 render + 1 video seek (was 50 + 50). All 4 scrub paths (grab-scrub, scroll-scrub, drag-scrub, momentum) point at it. (E) The iOS resize STORM (address bar / keyboard sliding fires dozens of resize events/sec, each re-rasterizing up-to-8192px filmstrips) is now debounced to ONE trailing timeline rebuild. Verified: 25 resize events → 1 rebuild (was 25). (C) Scratch-canvas realloc guards — reassigning canvas.width/height every frame reallocates the full-res backing buffer; wrapped 13 offscreen buffers (pixel-effect ping-pong, group flatten, mask, motion-blur, rgbsplit/posterize/tint/threshold/duotone/mirror/pixelate, colour-adjust, camera) in an "only if the dims changed" guard — each still clears itself before drawing, verified a 3-effect stack renders identical non-blank output across consecutive frames (no stale-buffer garbage). Verified at 375px, console clean. STILL TO COME: Fix A (downscale preview canvas to on-screen size — the biggest lever) = Phase 2; effect/group static-frame caching = Phase 3.
- v2.85 — PARITY RE-AUDIT + three fixes. The parity matrix (generated 2026-06-23) had gone badly stale across v2.71→v2.84, so twelve agents re-verified every open row against the actual code, evidence required for each status. 251 rows re-checked, 120 were WRONG: 196 ✅ / 52 🟡 / 93 ❌ (was 90/62/189); core tier is 87/109 = 80% done. Two headline corrections: the effects catalog is not "shallow" (it has ~180 registered effects — blurs, glows, warps, faux-3D meshes, halftone, clouds, edges; nearly the whole distortion/stylize family the old doc listed as absent has shipped), and the app is not "unusable on touch/mobile" (6 @media blocks, touch-action:none on every drag surface, pinch-zoom, long-press reorder, bottom-sheet inspector). BACKLOG.md rebuilt from the true gaps — 9 of its 12 "quick wins" and 6 of its 18 bugs were already done. FIXES: (1) SOLO now silences audio in preview, not just picture — soloing a clip used to leave every other layer audible while editing, then the exported MP4 contained only the soloed audio (preview and export disagreed); a shared FM.soloSilenced() gate now mirrors the exporter's, verified to agree with it exactly. (2) EXPORT hands the finished MP4 to the OS share sheet where available (navigator.share with a File — Save to Photos / AirDrop / send), falling back to the old anchor-download when file-sharing is unsupported or the long export outlived its transient activation; a dismissed sheet does NOT then silently download. NOTE: desktop Chrome reports canShare(files)=false so it takes the fallback — the share path can only be confirmed on your phone. (3) SPEED now clamps duration against the source that's actually left, (srcDur − trimStart) / speed, exactly as the trim grips already do — a clip whose span overran its source used to freeze on its last decoded frame for the tail. REGRESSION FOUND (not yet fixed): the SOLO BUTTON is gone from the UI — the engine still honours layer.solo (compositor + exporter) but timeline.js/index.html have zero th-solo references and only ai-ops.js writes it, so solo is unreachable by hand; styles.css still carries 4 orphaned .th-solo rules. It was almost certainly lost in the mobile timeline rebuild. This also disproves the audit's own assumption that a ✅ feature can't un-ship, so the ~196 ✅ rows have never been re-verified and may hide other regressions. Verified at 380px, console clean.
- v2.86 — NINE NEW EFFECTS + a health-check of all 154 existing ones. NEW (all AM-parity gaps verified absent on 2026-07-10, each a self-contained pixel/warp fn + registry entry): Soft Glow (wide low-threshold bloom — bright-pass → separable blur → screen composite, radius scales with frame size), Replace Color (swap one hue for another: From/To colours + tolerance window with soft falloff, saturation/value preserved), Spot Color (keep one hue, desaturate the rest — sin-city look), Four-Color Gradient (bilinear blend of four corner colours — the inspector + registry gained generic color3/color4 picker support, so future 4-colour effects are free), Spectral Map (luminance → spectrum sweep, violet shadows to red highlights), Radial Shadow (point-light shadow with Light X/Y + Reach — marches each pixel back toward the light), Voronoi Cells (stained-glass mosaic, deterministic jittered-grid seeds so preview and export match, Edge darkening), Tunnel (radial-inversion warp), and two new Channel Remap modes (Hue Invert, Swap Sat/Val). Turbulent Displace was deliberately DROPPED: the shipped Fractal Warp already IS noise displacement — a duplicate would be bloat. HEALTH CHECK: automated smoke test rendered every one of the 154 existing effects on shape + text layers — zero errors; the five apparent no-ops (mirror, lightglow, darkglow, dropshadow, letterbox…) all turned out to be test artifacts (a centred shape mirrors to itself; pure white can't glow brighter) and were proven working by direct output scans. THREE REAL BUGS FIXED along the way: (1) VIGNETTE was a silent no-op on text/shape/path/group layers — it only ever drew inside the media branch; non-media layers now route through a new comp-space pixel pass (media keeps its clip-bounds draw, unchanged). (2) LONGSHADOW smeared shadow from the canvas edge along every diagonal before it had seen any content — the run counter now arms only after content. (3) The Dreamy preset wrote blur {amount:6} but blur's param key is radius — silently ignored (masked because the default is also 6). Also guarded the warp-effect scratch canvases against per-frame reallocation (the pair v2.84's pass missed). NOTE: script cache-busting — index.html pins each JS file with ?v=N; compositor/fx-registry/inspector bumped (155/31/143). Verified: all 9 new effects render (pixel-diff per effect), 159-effect regression sweep clean, four-colour heart + radial shadow + voronoi text screenshot at 380px, console clean.
- v2.87 — THREE FEATURES you asked for. (1) SLIDERS HIT A WALL at min/max: the effect drag-strips used to keep "sliding" past a limit — the value stuck but the ribbed texture kept scrolling, and reversing had a dead zone (you had to retrace the whole overshoot before the value moved again). Now the drag re-anchors at the limit — the texture freezes dead at the wall and the instant you reverse, the value responds. Fixed in all FOUR drag-scrub controls (effect sliders, Border & Shadow rows, the Move & Transform value boxes, and the Scale/Skew tick strips). Verified with synthetic pointer drags: 2000px past max → pinned at max, texture frozen; reverse 30px → value drops by exactly the expected amount, instantly. (2) EXPORT A MARKED REGION OR ONE CLIP: the export dialog's Range menu gains "Selected clip only" (exports exactly the selected clip's time span) plus a "Hide other layers (export just this clip)" checkbox that temporarily solos the clip — picture AND audio — and restores everything after, even if the export fails or is cancelled. The ⋯ More menu gains Mark export start / Mark export end / Clear export marks so the region can be set BY TOUCH (the [ and ] keys were keyboard-only — phones had no way to mark a range). Both range picks verified end-to-end: clip at 2s–5s exports 2→5 with only it soloed during the run and everything restored after; controls grey out when nothing is selected. (3) REMOVE OBJECT (touch-up) — new effect that erases watermarks/subtitles/anything in a box: draws over the region with content pulled in from its surroundings (the same border-interpolation algorithm ffmpeg's delogo filter uses), plus Blur and Mosaic fill modes and a Feather control. The region (x/y/w/h) is KEYFRAMEABLE, so it can track a moving watermark. Comes with a "Select area on canvas" button that opens a drag-a-box overlay (crop-tool style — drag to move, handles to resize, draw fresh on empty space) instead of nudging four sliders on a phone; Done commits to history, Cancel restores, works with touch. Featured second in the effects carousel. Verified: a synthetic 1623-white-pixel WATERMARK burned into a gradient frame → 0 white pixels after, and the patched centre matches the surrounding gradient almost exactly; Blur/Mosaic modes clean; picker opens, drags (x 30→40 for a 10%-width drag) and closes on Done. HONEST NOTE: this is a local content-aware patch, not generative AI — your Anthropic key can't inpaint video, and this runs free/offline and exports identically. It shines on even-ish backgrounds (exactly the subtitle/watermark case); busy textures will show smoothing. Cache-busters bumped: app 136, inspector 144, compositor 156, fx-registry 32, + new touchup-tool.js.
- v2.88 — EVERY EFFECT HAS A PICTURE, and the moving ones MOVE. The Add-Effect browser's tiles and featured cards now show a real preview of what each effect does — and there are no image files behind it: the app renders its own thumbnails by applying the REAL effect to a tiny built-in sample scene (a teal circle + amber dot on a dark gradient, 96px). Effects that move — shake, wiggle, swing, spin, pulse, drift, orbit, the wipes and dissolves, glitch, glow scan, light leak, noise, counters, motion blur (content) and more (26 detected) — get a looping ANIMATED preview: exactly the "circle shaking around" you described, but rendered live from the actual effect code, so every future effect gets its picture automatically and previews can never drift out of sync with what the effect really does. Animation is AUTO-DETECTED (render two moments in time, compare pixels), previews stream in one per frame so the browser never stutters (~3s for all 163 on desktop), play at 11fps from cached frame strips, and stop the instant the browser closes. Per-effect tuning where defaults lied: drift/orbit's 1080p-scale motion flung the sample clean off the 96px frame (both showed empty background — caught by an adversarial sweep that hashed all 163 thumbs and flagged blanks/duplicates), darkglow had no dark pixels to glow, replacecolor's default From-colour wasn't in the sample, and two twin pairs (threshold≡solid matte, temperature≡color balance) were nudged apart. After the fixes: 163/163 render, all visually unique, 26 animating, console clean, verified at 380px (featured carousel + category grids). ALSO: the featured carousel now leads with the newest effects (Remove Object, Voronoi, Soft Glow, Four-Color Gradient…) — standing rule from Ezra, remembered for every future update. And vignette is no longer wrongly blocked from text/shape layers in the add-flow (v2.86 made it render everywhere; the gate hadn't caught up). Cache-busters: fx-registry 33, fx-browser 7, styles 170, + new fx-thumbs.js.
- v2.89 — REFRESH KEEPS YOUR PLACE. Reloading the page (or tapping the version label to force-update) used to always dump you on the project-browser home screen, even though your project data was fully restored behind it. The app now remembers which screen you were on (home.js writes 'fm.view' to localStorage on every home open/close) and the boot path skips the home overlay when you were last in the editor — refresh mid-edit and you're straight back in your project. Going home and refreshing still shows home; first boot still shows home; and if the remembered project can't be restored (deleted, fresh install) it falls back to home rather than opening an empty editor. The force-update reset is covered too: it nukes the service worker + caches but never localStorage, and it reloads on a ?fresh= URL, which doesn't matter since the flag isn't URL-based. Verified: editor→reload→editor, home→reload→home, console clean.
- v2.90 — shapes spawn in a RANDOM COLOUR each time. Every shape added from the Add menu (and freehand/vector drawings, which ride the same path) gets its own vivid random colour — random hue with saturation and lightness kept in a range that never lands on mud, near-black or washed-out grey. Creation-time only: saved projects, imports, templates, duplicates and AI-built scenes keep their stored colours, and the effect-thumbnail sample scenes are unaffected (they set their own fills). Verified through the real spawn path: consecutive shapes come out different (lime, orange, blue, purple…), explicit fills still respected.
- v2.91 — effects-editor polish, three fixes you reported. (1) EFFECT ROWS ARE AN ACCORDION now, like Blending & Opacity: opening one effect's editor closes every other, so exactly one is ever open — and ADDING an effect from the browser lands with the new effect's controls already expanded, ready to tweak. (2) COLOUR PICKING NO LONGER CLOSES YOUR MENU: the eyedropper (and the crop / Remove-Object box tools) put a full-screen overlay over the app, and the tap to sample a colour landed on that overlay — which the "tap empty space to deselect" handler read as background, so it deselected the layer and the panel vanished mid-pick. All tool overlays are now on that handler's keep-list; sampling a colour keeps you exactly where you were. (3) BORDER ON A DRAWING GOES UNDER IT: on freehand/vector drawings (and lines/arcs — anything stroked rather than filled), enabling Border used to swap the whole line to the border colour — the border painted OVER the drawing and the shadow followed the border. A border is now a 2×-width under-stroke in the border colour with the drawing's own colour on top (the same trick filled shapes use for "outside" borders), and the shadow is cast once by the outer silhouette instead of doubling up. Verified pixel-exact: line centre = drawing colour, border ring flanks it, single clean shadow; accordion and auto-open driven through the real UI; eyedropper tap keeps the selection; console clean.
- v2.92 — THE SLIDER REBUILD + CANVAS PAN/ZOOM + effect page arrows. (1) SLIDERS ARE REAL RULERS now, Alight-style: every effect / Border & Shadow slider is a full-width strip of vertical tick notches that scrolls under the fixed green value line. The notches are REAL SNAP POINTS (drag lands exactly on them, like timeline frame-snap — typed values stay free-form), the ends are the actual bounds (solid white wall markers — drag stops dead, reverses instantly), and brighter white lines mark values you care about: the midpoint, zero, the effect's default, and every 45° on angle sliders (the notch ladder even picks degree-quantums that divide 45 so those landmarks stay landable). Root cause of the "PC abomination" found: the PC inspector is pinned at 286px and the old single-line layout left the actual slider ~23px wide — rows are now two-line (label row over a full-width ruler), which is also exactly AM's layout. PC Move & Transform strips got the same tick look. (2) CANVAS: dragging can no longer steal your selection — with a layer selected, dragging anywhere moves THAT layer only (a stationary tap off it still deselects; a tap on it still reopens the phone sheet). With NOTHING selected, dragging grabs the whole preview and pans it, mouse-wheel or two-finger pinch zooms (0.2×–8×) — a pure view-level transform: never in undo, never saved, doesn't touch the timeline. Reset it from the canvas-size dialog's new "Reset view" row (with live zoom % readout), or it resets itself when you go home / switch projects. Bonus: this rework fixed a pre-existing selection-box misalignment under the old zoom buttons. The box tools (crop, Remove Object, edit points, tracker, drawing) reset the view when they open — their overlays don't yet understand a zoomed viewport (backlogged). (3) EFFECT PAGES HAVE ARROWS: every category view in the Add-Effect browser now has ‹ prev / next › buttons pinned at the bottom (with a 2/13 position readout) so you can page through all the effect categories in place instead of Back → pick → Back. Wraps at the ends. Verified end-to-end: synthetic drags (5 notches = exactly 5×q landing on-grid, wall at exactly max, instant reverse), two-overlapping-layers drag keeps selection + moves only the selected one, pan/zoom/reset transforms verified, arrows page both directions without view stacking, 375px screenshots. NOTE: preview-pane rAF throttling made thumbnails crawl during testing — environmental only, real browsers generate at full speed.
- v2.93 — the canvas never selects. Completing the v2.92 interaction model: with nothing selected, TAPPING the preview now selects nothing at all (it used to still pick the layer under your finger) — the canvas is purely the player surface: tap does nothing, drag pans, pinch/wheel zooms. Layers are picked from the timeline. Everything else unchanged and re-verified: drag still pans the viewport, a timeline-selected layer still drags (selection can't be stolen), tap OFF the selected layer still deselects, tap ON it still reopens the phone sheet.
- v2.94 — SIX MORE EFFECTS from your Alight Motion screenshots (the genuinely-missing ones — I skipped the ~30 you already have, e.g. Kaleidoscope, Squeeze, Pinch/Bulge, Voronoi, Soft Glow, Radial Shadow, Spot Color, all the 3D solids). NEW: Turbulent Displace (organic domain-warped noise churn that boils over time — distinct from the existing Fractal Warp's plain sine warp), Stretch Segment (grab a horizontal band and pull it — position/band/stretch controls), Tile Shift (chop into tiles and offset alternate rows/columns — the sliced-glitch look), Tile Rotate (spin each tile's content about its own centre, neighbours counter-rotate → woven pinwheel), Palette Map (quantise to a small N-colour palette — banded screen-print look; this was on the backlog), and Lightning (procedural jagged bolts that flicker over time, with Bolts/Intensity/Colour — additive glow, no random so preview and export match). Catalog is now 169 effects. All six render distinct non-blank output verified pixel-by-pixel, Turbulent Displace + Lightning animate, and they lead the featured carousel (newest-first). NOT added and why: Displacement Map / Polar Displacement Map need a SECOND layer as the displacement source (real pipeline work, not a self-contained effect) — backlogged; Omino Glass / Omino Diffusion+ are branded plugin looks we approximate with the existing Glass effect; Simple Starfield duplicates the Starfield we already have. Verified at 375px (Lightning bolts + Tile-Rotate tiling screenshot-confirmed on the real stage), console clean.
- v2.95 — DISPLACEMENT MAP + POLAR DISPLACEMENT — the "hard" ones I'd backlogged, now built properly. These are the first effects that warp a layer using ANOTHER layer's pixels, so they needed real new machinery, not just a formula: a source-layer PICKER (new effect-param type — a dropdown of the other layers in your project), a dedicated render path that renders the chosen map layer to its own buffer and pushes each pixel of your layer by the map's colour there, and a recursion guard so two layers displacing each other can't lock up. Displacement Map offsets X/Y by the map (Red→X, Green→Y, or Luminance for both); Polar Displacement offsets in radius + angle for ripples/whirls. Pick any layer as the map — a Clouds/Noise/Turbulence layer makes flowing organic warps, a gradient makes a smooth lens. With no map chosen it self-displaces by its own brightness. A stale/deleted map id safely falls back to self; the map layer can stay hidden (it only drives the warp, never shows). Catalog is now 171 effects. Verified: base vs self vs map-driven vs polar all render distinct pixels; two layers cross-referencing each other don't hang (depth-guarded); a bogus source id doesn't crash; the picker lists the other layers and excludes self/camera/null; visible stripe-warp on the real stage; console clean. NOTE: heavy effect (renders two full layers + a per-pixel pass) — fine as an opt-in, and it'll get cheaper once the preview-downscale perf work lands.
- v2.96 — effect sliders drag the other way now (you asked). The AM-style tick rulers (effects + Border & Shadow) now scroll like you're grabbing the ruler and pushing it: drag LEFT to raise the value (a higher notch slides under the fixed centre line), drag RIGHT to lower it — i.e. you drag against the direction you want the value to go, matching Alight Motion. The wall-at-the-ends, notch-snapping and value markers are all unchanged. Verified with synthetic drags: drag-left 0→+150, drag-right 0→−150, console clean.
- v2.97 — PROJECT BROWSER: multi-select, bulk actions, duplicate — AND a real fix for the "too many projects = lag" problem. (1) A "Select" button (top bar) or ⋯ → "Select…" enters multi-select: tick any number of project cards (teal ring + check), then a bottom bar does Delete, Duplicate, or Select-all on all of them at once — no more deleting one by one. (2) Duplicate is on every project's ⋯ menu (makes a full copy incl. its media), and now also works in bulk from select mode. (3) THE LAG QUESTION — yes, our version COULD have hit the same wall Alight Motion does, and I found exactly why: the project list was one localStorage blob with every project's thumbnail baked in as ~8KB of base64, and that whole blob got re-parsed and re-written on EVERY autosave (~every 0.6s while you edit). At a few hundred projects that's multiple megabytes rewritten per save = the lag, plus it eventually hits the storage quota and autosave silently stops. FIXED at the source: thumbnails now live in IndexedDB (huge quota, never touched by autosave), so the project index is ~130 bytes per project instead of ~8KB — measured 653 bytes for 5 projects. It'll stay fast into the hundreds now. A one-time migration lifts your existing thumbnails across automatically on first open. There's also a gentle count note on the home screen once you pass 60 projects — informational (tap Select to tidy up), never the "you must delete to keep working" nag. Verified: thumb lands in IDB not the index, duplicate copies it, delete removes it, bulk delete of 2/4 works, select mode toggles clean, migration flag sets, console clean.
- v2.98 — PROJECT ⋯ MENU FIXED + a much better layer-reorder drag. (1) The three-dots on each project card wasn't opening its menu (Open / Rename / Duplicate / Select / Save as template / Export / Delete) — the card was a <button> with the ⋯ <button> nested inside it, which is invalid HTML and silently breaks the inner tap on iOS Safari. Cards are now <div>s, so the ⋯ opens reliably and everything in it (duplicate, delete, select) works. (2) LAYER REORDER got three upgrades you asked for: (a) AUTO-SCROLL — drag a layer to the top or bottom edge of the list and it keeps scrolling the timeline for you, so you can move a layer across a long list in one continuous drag instead of drag-stop-scroll-regrab; (b) INSERTION LINE — a bright line now shows between clips exactly where the layer will drop, so there's no guessing; (c) MULTI-DRAG — select several layers (the same paint-select you use on the left) and drag them ALL together in one go, preserving their order. That last one isn't even in Alight Motion — a little something of our own. Verified: ⋯ opens with all items; single drag up & down lands exactly on the insertion line; multi-select drags as a block preserving order; drop-in-place is a no-op (no stray undo step); console clean.
- v2.99 — multi-drag now VISUALISES, and Move & Transform keeps things aligned. (1) When you drag several selected layers to reorder, ALL of them now lift and move with your finger together (before, only the top one followed and the rest just dimmed in place) — so you can see exactly what you're carrying. (2) ALIGNMENT SNAPPING, shared between canvas dragging and Move & Transform: both now snap to the composition CENTRE, the edges, AND the positions this layer sits at in its OTHER keyframes — so you can re-align something to exactly where it was before. Move & Transform's X/Y had no snapping at all before; now scrubbing them sticks at those alignment points and flashes the matching guide line on the canvas, which is the whole reason M&T is better for precise alignment. (3) Dragging on the canvas now updates the Move & Transform X/Y numbers LIVE as you move (they used to only catch up when you let go, so it looked like canvas dragging wasn't touching them). Note: canvas dragging an animated layer already creates/updates a keyframe at the playhead — verified — so it does drive the animation; if there's a specific keyframe case that still feels off, flag it. Verified: 3 selected rows translate together; align targets include keyframe values (300/777 in a test); canvas + M&T both snap to centre and previous keyframes and unsnap when far; canvas drag live-syncs M&T; console clean.
- v3.00 — canvas dragging no longer drops keyframes (your clarification). Dragging a layer around on the canvas now MOVES THE WHOLE ANIMATION instead of adding a keyframe at the playhead: if the layer has position (or scale/rotation) keyframes, every keyframe shifts by the same amount so the motion stays intact and just sits in a new spot — no stray keyframe, no messed-up timing. Move & Transform is unchanged: its X/Y/scrubs still keyframe precisely at the playhead, which is exactly what it's for. So the two tools now have distinct jobs — canvas = reposition the whole thing quickly, Move & Transform = precise per-keyframe animation. (Non-animated layers still just move their base position as before.) Verified through a real multi-step canvas drag: a 2-keyframe path (300→800) dragged +120px became (420→920) — both shifted together, zero keyframes added; M&T still adds a keyframe when you scrub it; console clean.
- v3.01 — moving a clip in time now carries its keyframes with it. Keyframe times are stored in absolute project time, so sliding a layer's clip earlier/later on the timeline used to leave its whole animation behind at the old time (the motion would play at the wrong moment, or freeze on its last frame inside the clip). Now, when you drag a clip's body to reposition it in time, every keyframe on that layer — transform, effects, volume/speed/fill/stroke/shadow — shifts by the same amount, so the animation rides along and stays perfectly in sync with the clip. Works for a single clip and for a multi-selected group dragged together (each shifts by its own delta), and it's one undo step. Verified: a layer with x-keyframes at t=0.5/1.5s dragged +2s on the timeline now has them at t=2.5/3.5s and animates in its new spot; console clean.
- v3.02 — layer-reorder (restacking) drag is now calm and controllable. The auto-scroll that kicks in when you drag a layer to the top/bottom edge of the list was way too fast and screen-dependent: it moved a fixed 15px PER FRAME, so ~900px/s on a 60Hz screen and ~1800px/s on a 120Hz one, ramping to full speed the instant you touched the edge — you'd overshoot every time. Rebuilt it: speed is now measured in px per SECOND (identical feel on any screen), capped at 520px/s (well under half the old top speed), and eased — it creeps gently right at the edge and only speeds up as you push further in, with sub-pixel accumulation so the slow end stays smooth instead of stuttering. Reordering across a long list is now precise. Verified: single + multi-layer reorder still land exactly on the insertion line; edge auto-scroll ramps smoothly and stops the instant you pull back; console clean.
- v3.03 — the reorder drag got the full iOS treatment: rows now PART to open a real animated gap where your layer will land (the insertion line is retired — the gap itself shows the drop spot, no guessing); a multi-selection stacks into one tight block under your finger and drops as a unit; drop targeting is computed from geometry snapshotted at grab-time so the animations can mathematically never confuse the hit-testing; the drag now survives a mid-drag timeline rebuild (autosave/playback) by re-acquiring its rows; pointercancel (browser steals the touch) now CANCELS instead of half-applying a move; and a subtle haptic tick on Android when the drop slot changes. Also app-wide: the undo system now skips identical snapshots, so a no-op action can never add a stray undo step anywhere. Stress-tested: 200-drag fuzz (random drags, taps, cancels, edge autoscrolls) — zero stuck rows/classes, zero order corruption; 30 targeted multi-drags — blocks always land adjacent and in order; single drop lands on the exact expected row; mid-drag rebuild keeps visuals AND applies the right drop; verified at 380px with a mid-flight screenshot; console clean.
- v3.04 — TIMELINE HARDENING, round 1 of the deep audit (multi-agent audit of the whole codebase; 24 findings so far, this batch fixes the timeline's 12). THE CRITICAL ONE: trimming a speed-RAMPED video set its duration to NaN and collapsed the entire timeline (an animated speed prop is an object, and the trim math divided by it) — fixed with proper ramp-aware maths (exact integral clamp at the source end), NaN guards so a non-finite number can never reach the scene, plus every other raw speed call-site fixed (export audio, reversed audio, media relink, reset-speed menu, clip badges — ramped clips now show a ⚡ramp badge). ALSO: pinch-zooming the timeline no longer rebuilds EVERYTHING per finger-move (rAF-throttled + filmstrips reuse their cached canvas mid-pinch and re-crispen once on release — it used to re-rasterize up to 8192px-wide canvases per clip at 120Hz, freezing iPhones); filmstrip/waveform canvases are now cached across rebuilds entirely (every tap used to re-allocate ~1MB per video clip); the ruler no longer emits one notch-div per FRAME on long projects (capped, so a 10-min video renders ~1500 not 18,000); cancelled gestures (pinch-start / browser-stolen touch) now RESTORE what they'd half-moved instead of leaving clips silently desynced from their keyframes; rebuilds are deferred while a drag or the marker-rename input is active (fixes the frozen keyframe-diamond drag and the marker name being silently wiped); phone solo-view can no longer accidentally send the layer to the back via a 4px wiggle on its ≡ handle; touch hold-to-move now carries the whole multi-selection like desktop; keyframe diamonds got a ~35px invisible touch pad + press-and-hold opens the easing menu on iPhone (with a Delete item — right-click/dbl-click never existed on touch); paint-select backtrack no longer triggers a rebuild storm per row. Verified: NaN repro now finite (4.93s trim on a ramped clip, ⚡ramp badge); ramp integral maths exact (bisection = analytic 2.472s); pointercancel restores clip start AND keyframes; solo wiggle no longer reorders; 120-gesture mixed fuzz (reorders/moves/trims/kf-drags + cancels + edge scrolls) — zero corruption, zero stuck state; console clean.
- v3.05 — CANVAS HARDENING, audit round 2 (8 fixes, all verified in-browser). (1) Two-finger safety: a second finger during a corner-handle drag now correctly becomes a viewport pinch instead of silently spawning a second drag that BOTH fingers then fought over; every drag is owned by the finger that started it (a stray touch can neither hijack nor end it); a third finger is ignored outright (it used to freeze the pinch and make the zoom JUMP when you lifted a finger). (2) Pinch-zoom is now FINGER-ANCHORED: the content under your fingertips stays under them while you zoom (verified drift: 0.00px) — it used to zoom about the canvas centre so the spot you were zooming into slid away; PC mouse-wheel zoom same, anchored at the cursor. (3) Scaling an animated layer from a corner handle now scales the whole animation MULTIPLICATIVELY — the additive shift could push other scale keyframes NEGATIVE (mirrored, inside-out rendering mid-animation). (4) The self-snap ratchet is gone: dragging a keyframed layer snapped against its own live-moving keyframe values, so slow drags stuck and jumped in ~14px steps — align targets are now snapshotted at grab time (verified: buttery exact finger tracking). (5) Locked layers can no longer be scaled/rotated via the handles (lock now means locked). (6) Grabbing anything on the canvas pauses playback — dragging a moving layer mid-play used to smear its whole keyframe path by the animation's own motion. (7) Selection handles counter-scale against viewport zoom, so they stay finger-sized at 0.2× (were ~5px, untappable) and don't swallow small layers at 8×. (8) The Move&Transform guide auto-hide timer can no longer blank the snap guides in the middle of a live canvas drag. All verified: multiplicative kf scale (0.5→2.0 rescaled to 0.075→0.3, no negatives), locked handle inert, foreign-finger moves ignored + drag survives foreign lift, pinch drift 0.00px, third finger ignored with pinch alive, pause-on-grab, corridor drag tracks exactly; console clean.
- v3.06 — GROUPS & PARENTING geometry, audit round 3 (the two deep ones). (1) Rotating or scaling a GROUP from the canvas handles no longer flings its members off-frame: groups are stored with their transform origin at the project's top-left corner, so the handles used to orbit everything around (0,0) — they now pivot about the group's visible bounds centre, compensating the group's position every frame so the box turns/scales exactly in place (verified: a 92° rotation moved the members' true centre by 1px; before, ~2000px). Group dragging also snaps by its visible bounds centre now (snapping its internal offset to "composition centre" was meaningless). (2) PARENTED layers (layer linked to another via Parent, or group members in Edit Group after the group has moved) are finally first-class on the canvas: the selection box draws where the layer is actually rendered (it used to float detached by the parent's offset/rotation), tapping the layer on canvas actually hits it (tap used to deselect because the hit region was at the un-parented coords), and dragging maps the finger through the parent's rotation/scale so the layer follows the finger exactly (dragging a child of a 90°-rotated parent used to slide it sideways). One shared parent-chain walk, mirroring the compositor's transform math exactly. Verified: box centre lands on the compositor's world position to the pixel; tap-on-layer keeps selection; +50px world drag = +50 local under a 90° parent; group rotate/scale pivot in place; console clean.
- v3.07 — MULTI-SELECT bar rebuilt to match Alight Motion (your screenshot). The canvas "page align" buttons (align left/centre/right/top/middle/bottom + distribute) are GONE from multi-select. In their place: (1) EDIT N CLIPS — trim-starts-to-playhead, SPLIT-all-at-playhead, trim-ends-to-playhead, and a red DELETE that removes the whole selection in one tap (and one undo step restores them all); the trim/split buttons grey out until the playhead is inside at least one selected clip, and each acts on every selected clip it crosses. (2) ALIGN ON TIMELINE — three buttons that move clips in TIME only (canvas position untouched): "start together" (all clips begin at the earliest start), "one after another" (each clip starts where the previous ends, in timeline row order), "end together" (all clips finish at the latest end). Keyframes ride along with every time shift, same rule as clip dragging. The single-layer trim/split row no longer renders alongside (it only acted on the primary layer — a confusing near-duplicate). Also fixed in passing: the single-layer "trim start to playhead" used raw layer.speed arithmetic — NaN on a speed-ramped clip (same family as the v3.04 critical; inspector.js was missed in that sweep). Verified: page-align gone; start-together [0,0,0] with keyframes shifted correctly; sequence [0,2,3.5]; end-together all at 4.5; split-all 3→6 clips all cut exactly at the playhead; trim-starts/ends exact; delete-all clears selection and ONE undo restores everything; one undo step per action; 380px phone screenshot matches the AM layout; console clean.
- v3.08 — TIMELINE GESTURES, audit round 4 (14 fixes from the second fleet run). The big one: a mid-drag rebuild (async filmstrip arriving, resize) could destroy the reorder drag's captured handle — the drag silently died AND its auto-scroll loop kept running forever, pinning the layer list against an edge until reload; reorder drags now defer rebuilds like every other gesture. Also: a second thumb resting on the timeline mid-reorder no longer converts the drag into a pinch (and a pinch's second finger landing on a clip can no longer select a random clip or start a stray scrub on release); reordering a GROUP's row now carries its members' stacking slots with it (moving just the group layer changed nothing on screen — members are what actually render); in Edit Group, dropping a member at the bottom slot now lands at the bottom of the GROUP, not the bottom of the entire project; LOCKED layers are now genuinely locked in the timeline too (no reorder, no time-move, no trim, no keyframe drag — they were fully editable before, defeating the lock); vertically panning the layer list no longer counts as a "tap" that wipes your multi-selection on release; in select-mode, tapping a clip's big BAR now toggles membership like tapping its head (it used to collapse the whole painted selection); multi-drags with a small start offset no longer ratchet (co-dragged clips were their own snap targets — a feedback loop); keyframe-diamond finger jitter below the drag threshold no longer nudges keyframes by a frame while the long-press menu is arming; trim grips and kf-dots take pointer capture (drags survive the cursor leaving the window) and ignore right-clicks; the ruler's major ticks got the same hard cap as the frame notches (thousands of divs on long projects at deep zoom); a header long-press that spans a rebuild can no longer fire phantom select-mode. Verified: group reorder TGmmT→GmmTT with members riding; locked layer inert to move+reorder; vertical pan keeps selection; 40-drag fuzz zero problems; console clean.
- v3.09 — DATA-SAFETY & UNDO, audit round 5 (13 fixes; two CRITICALS). (1) Deleting a layer then pressing UNDO no longer brings it back permanently BLANK — delete was destroying the layer's media (registry + IndexedDB blob) on the spot, so the undone layer had nothing to show and the footage was gone for good; media now survives deletion (genuinely orphaned blobs are still reaped by the existing boot-time sweep). (2) Curved text with a border CRASHED every render (a ReferenceError — the arc renderer read stroke variables from another function's scope); fixed, curved+bordered text renders. Also: editing any value while PLAYING now pauses playback first — scrubbing an animated property mid-play sprayed a new keyframe every frame at the advancing playhead, shredding the animation; undo inside Edit Group no longer strands you in a deleted group's empty timeline; undo snapshots now strip runtime flags (undoing could resurrect a stale "crop editor open" flag that silently disabled a layer's crop); the undo stack is capped by SIZE as well as count (120 snapshots of a huge scene ≈ hundreds of MB on an iPhone); a quick Cmd+Z right after a camera wheel-zoom no longer discards two edits at once (the zoom's trailing commit is flushed first); scaling an animated layer whose current scale is ~0 (a pop-in's first frame) no longer explodes the other keyframes to enormous values (falls back to additive); CROP keyframes are now first-class (they were invisible to every keyframe system: clip moves left crop animation behind, diamonds couldn't delete them); Copy/Paste and template/element insertion now RETIME keyframes to the playhead (pasted/inserted animation used to play at its old absolute time, outside the new clip); hold-easing evaluated AT the keyframe returns the new value (snap-to-keyframe used to land on the OLD value); the graph editor no longer silently edits the LAST segment when the playhead sits a hair past the first keyframe. Verified: curved+border renders; delete→undo restores layer WITH media; hold at t=2 gives 99 (10 at 1.99); paste at t=5 lands kfs [5.2, 6.5]; live edit pauses playback; console clean.
- v3.10 — STORAGE HARDENING, audit round 6 (8 fixes). The embarrassing one: the boot-time orphan sweep was DELETING EVERY PROJECT THUMBNAIL on every launch (the 'thumb:' prefix wasn't in its keep-list) — they silently regenerated when you next opened each project, which is why the home screen sometimes showed placeholders; thumbnails now persist, and only a deleted project's thumb is really pruned. Also: each browser tab now pins its saves to the project it LOADED — two tabs open on different projects used to make one tab's autosave silently overwrite the other project's file (the save key was re-read from shared storage at write time); one corrupt/undecodable media blob no longer aborts the restore of every layer after it (per-layer isolation); the orphan sweep's keep-list is now built from EVERY stored project doc, not just the index — a project that missed indexing (quota hiccup) can no longer have all its media mass-deleted at boot; Duplicate on the currently-open project flushes the last ~600ms of edits first (the copy could silently miss your newest change) and registers the copy in the index BEFORE the slow media copying (killing the tab mid-copy used to strand an invisible project that the sweep then gutted); failed video/image imports revoke their object URLs (each retry of a big .mov pinned the whole file in memory); audio decoding closes its AudioContext on failure (iOS caps live contexts at ~4 — a few silent videos could kill ALL app audio); the "Storage full" toast fires once, not every 600ms, when only the index write is failing. Verified: live project's thumb survives pruneOrphans while a dead project's is removed; pinned save writes to the bound project; console clean.
- v3.11 — COMPOSITOR, audit round 7 (6 fixes). Stacking two per-pixel effects on one layer no longer leaves a ghost of the un-effected layer underneath (the nested render pass was reusing the outer pass's scratch canvas — alpha-cutting effects like Dissolve/Wipe simply stopped working when stacked; scratch buffers are now a recursion-depth pool, verified pixel-exact: dissolve@1+gamma renders identical to an empty scene); a vignetted clip now FADES its vignette with the layer's opacity (fading out used to leave a floating dark ring at full strength — verified 0 differing pixels at opacity 0); cropping a reversed or frame-blended clip samples the crop rectangle at the cache bitmap's real density (crop coords are full-source pixels but cached frames are downscaled ~960px — the crop showed the wrong region or blank); frame-blend slow-motion now engages for keyframed speed RAMPS (the object-vs-number comparison was always false, so ramped slow-mo stuttered on duplicated frames); a parent's animated scale is clamped like the layer's own (an overshoot ease through zero flipped every child inside-out for a frame); canvas-effect scratch buffers (wiggle/3D/tiles/…) stop reallocating their ~8MB backing stores every frame. Verified: stacked-fx pixel-diff 0; vignette-fade pixel-diff 0; layer-visible sanity 6936px; console clean.
- v3.12 — INSPECTOR & PHONE-SHEET, audit round 8 (9 fixes). On the phone, dragging DOWN on the Move trackpad / rotate dial / value scrubs no longer gets hijacked by the sheet's swipe-to-dismiss (it used to close the sheet and DESELECT the layer mid-gesture); an OS-cancelled swipe (notification banner, edge gesture) can no longer complete a swipe-DELETE on an effect row (cancel now aborts, only a real release commits); Paste Style re-anchors copied keyframes to the target clip's start (pasted animation used to sit at the source's absolute times — often entirely outside the target clip, looking frozen); the Move & Transform keyframe diamond decides add-vs-remove at CLICK time instead of build time (after scrubbing the playhead it silently no-opped or deleted the wrong keyframe); the quick-row Trim/Split buttons no longer freeze in their build-time disabled state (scrubbing into the clip left them grey and dead — they're always live now and guard themselves); colour-wheel and graph-editor drags commit on pointercancel (an interrupted drag left uncommitted changes that the next unrelated action swept into ITS undo step, and the curve editor's next touch silently rewrote the curve). Verified: split no-ops with the playhead outside the clip and splits 1→2 inside it from the same (enabled) button; console clean.
- v3.13 — the reorder drop now SETTLES: releasing a drag glides the lifted block into its open gap over 150ms before the list reorders underneath (it used to teleport into place the instant you let go — the last non-native-feeling moment in the reorder gesture). Cancelled drags still snap back instantly and never apply. Verified: block animates mid-settle, lands on the exact expected row, all transient classes/transforms cleared after; console clean.
- v3.14 — deferred-list cleanup while the final 9 auditors run (7 fixes). Typing an exact X/Y value into Move & Transform now STICKS (typed 545 was silently snapped to centre 540 and rounded — snap now applies only to scrub drags, where it belongs); media-fill images are bounded + handle corrupt data (the decode cache grew unbounded all session and a bad fill image retried forever); Edit Points panels no longer accumulate a dead change-listener per inspector refresh; effect Copy/Paste and saved presets strip runtime UI flags (pasting could leave two accordion editors open at once and bloat saves); bezier handles in the easing editor clamp to the visible graph (a strong overshoot dragged the handle off-canvas where it became invisible and ungrabbable); removed the dead index-based reorder API (one reorder entry point now). Also fixed a self-inflicted parse error from this batch's tooling (a comment swallowed a preset-save statement) — caught by the boot check before commit. Verified: typed 545 sticks; presets strip _expanded; dead API gone; console clean.
- v3.15 — APP-CORE & selection, audit round 8b (10 fixes, incl. a critical + several data-safety). CRITICAL: keyboard Delete/Backspace was STILL destroying a clip's media (registry entry + IndexedDB blob) — so Delete-then-Undo brought the clip back permanently blank with the footage gone (I'd only fixed the menu-delete path in v3.09; the keyboard path was missed). Now Delete keeps the media so undo restores it. Also: (1) after Duplicate / Split / adding a clip, the selection SET (selectedIds) stayed pointing at the OLD layer, so the timeline highlighted two rows and pressing Delete removed the WRONG one — now the new layer is the sole selection. (2) Keyboard-Delete on a group now cascades to its members (they used to be orphaned, left pointing at a dead group). (3) Duplicating a GROUP now brings its members — before you got an empty, invisible "Group copy". (4) Splitting a clip now DIVIDES its keyframes at the cut (each half keeps only its own, with a seamless boundary keyframe) instead of both halves carrying the full set and showing stray diamonds outside their window. (5) Split validates the layer still exists after its async media reload (a delete/undo mid-split can't resurrect an orphan half). (6) Cmd+S / Cmd+M / Cmd+arrow are no longer hijacked — the Save reflex was silently SPLITTING the selected clip, Cmd+M dropping a marker; unhandled modifier combos now pass through to the browser. (7) Null / Camera / Adjustment / Caption layers added to an empty project get a usable 5s duration instead of 0 (they were invisible and un-editable). (8) Select-All and shift-click now refresh the full UI so the multi-select Group button/chrome appears. (9) deleteLayer validates the selection after a group cascade (no more phone zombie edit-mode on a deleted descendant). (10) The home-screen ⋯ menu is no longer invisible — it was painting BEHIND the home overlay (z-index 100 < 200), so Rename/Delete/Duplicate were untappable; raised to 300. Verified: dup selects only the copy & original survives Delete; group delete removes all 3; group duplicate → 2 groups × 2 members; delete→undo keeps media; split divides kfs [0,4]/[4,8] continuous at the cut; Cmd+S no-ops while plain S splits; ctx-menu z-index 300; console clean.
- v3.16 — COMPOSITOR EFFECTS, audit round 9 (4 fixes). Keyframing Chroma Key tolerance (or Luma Key threshold) no longer silently disables the key — the raw keyframe object was being fed into the pixel math as NaN, so animating the tolerance made the green-screen key just stop working; both now evaluate the value at the current time. Stacking two WARP effects (Wave + Twirl, any of the ~22 warps) no longer leaves an un-warped ghost of the layer bleeding through — same nested-scratch-canvas bug I fixed for pixel effects in v3.11, now fixed for warps with the same recursion-depth pool. An effect with a MISSING parameter (older project saved before the param existed, or a hand-edited/AI node) now falls back to its catalog default instead of 0 — Threshold in particular used to turn the whole layer solid white. And a canvas effect's second scratch buffer stopped reallocating its ~8MB backing store every single frame (the resize-guard its sibling got in v3.11). Verified: all 171 effects render at 2 time points (0 crashes), all 25 warp×warp stack combinations render clean, keyframed chroma key renders without NaN, threshold-with-no-param stays dark not white; console clean.
- v3.17 — EXPORT & media, audit round 10 (6 fixes incl. a critical). CRITICAL: exporting a project with a long reversed (or frame-blend slow-mo) 1080p/4K clip could OOM-kill mobile Safari — the export frame cache was unbounded (up to 900 full-res frames ≈ several GB).
